properly explain everything in detail that I will page and subpage on notion later ### Overall Project Goal You're building a comprehensive DIY home automation system centered around an ESP32 microcontroller for controlling audio volume (initially via Windows system or per-app, later integrated with a physical amplifier), LED lights (via relays), and an interactive LED matrix for creative lighting effects. The system emphasizes fail-safe features, like motorized potentiometers that automatically revert to a programmed default state (e.g., 0% volume) on power loss, reboot, or signal interruption to prevent issues like sudden loud audio or full-bright lights. Everything is modular and expandable: Starting with core audio/light controls, you're adding a touch screen for drawing on a serpentine LED matrix with effects (e.g., fades, ripples), a second ESP32 for waking your PC (via Wake-on-LAN or direct power button short), and an IR module for remote control. Control is multi-modal—via Windows software, local ESP32 processing, WiFi (e.g., MQTT), Bluetooth, or IR remote—making it a unified smart hub for your home setup. The design prioritizes affordability, maker-friendliness, and scalability, with future-proofing for amp integration. This setup turns your control panel into an intuitive interface: Twist knobs for volume/lights, draw on the screen for custom LED patterns, and use remotes or apps to trigger everything remotely or automatically. ### Core System: Audio and Light Control #### Purpose and Usage - \u003cstrong\u003eAudio Control\u003c/strong\u003e: Adjust Windows system volume or per-app (e.g., Spotify, browser) levels using motorized rotary pots. On signal loss (e.g., WiFi dropout or reboot), pots motor back to default (e.g., muted) to avoid blasts. Later, integrate with a DIY amp for analog audio control (e.g., speakers directly from the pot's signal gang). - \u003cstrong\u003eLight Control\u003c/strong\u003e: Switch or dim 12V LED lights (e.g., strips or bulbs) via relays. Use pots or relays for on/off/dimming, with fail-safe to off/low state. - \u003cstrong\u003eHow It Works\u003c/strong\u003e: ESP32 reads pot positions via ADC, drives motors for automated adjustments, and communicates with Windows for volume changes. Relays handle high-current switching. Usage: Manual knob twists for local control; remote via app/remote for automated scenes (e.g., \"movie mode\" dims lights and lowers volume). #### Components - \u003cstrong\u003eMicrocontroller\u003c/strong\u003e: ESP32-WROOM-32 DevKit (or similar, \u003cdel\u003e$5). Handles ADC for pots, PWM for motors, GPIOs for relays, and WiFi/BT for communication. - \u003cstrong\u003eMotorized Potentiometers\u003c/strong\u003e: - Primary: Alps RK27112MC (20KΩ log taper, dual-gang, 6-9V DC motor, \u003cdel\u003e$15-25 each). Use 1-2 for stereo/master volume (log taper for perceptual audio linearity). - Alternative: Bourns PRM162-K420K-103A2 (10KΩ log, \u003cdel\u003e$20-35) for smaller size. - Why dual-gang: One for feedback (ESP32 reads position), one for future amp signal. - \u003cstrong\u003eMotor Driver\u003c/strong\u003e: L298N or DRV8833 H-bridge module (\u003c/del\u003e$2-5 per pot). Controls motor direction/speed via ESP32 PWM. - \u003cstrong\u003eRelays\u003c/strong\u003e: 4-channel 12V relay module (e.g., Songle SRD-12VDC-SL-C, \u003cdel\u003e$5). Each channel switches a 12V LED circuit (up to 10A). For dimming, add MOSFETs (e.g., IRF540) with PWM. - \u003cstrong\u003ePower Supplies\u003c/strong\u003e: 5-9V 1A+ for ESP32/motors (e.g., wall adapter), 12V 5A+ for relays/LEDs. Common ground, separate regulators for noise isolation. - \u003cstrong\u003eLED Lights\u003c/strong\u003e: Your 12V LEDs (strips/bulbs, assume \u003cdel\u003e$10-20 setup). Connect via relays for on/off; MOSFET for dimming if needed. - \u003cstrong\u003eWiring Essentials\u003c/strong\u003e: Breadboard/perfboard for prototyping, wires, resistors (e.g., for H-bridge), knobs for pots (\u003c/del\u003e$1 each). #### Software and Integration - \u003cstrong\u003eESP32 Firmware\u003c/strong\u003e: Arduino IDE-based, using libraries like WiFi, PubSubClient (MQTT), USBHID (for direct Windows control). - Fail-safe: On boot or signal loss (e.g., MQTT timeout), drive pots to default position using proportional control loop. - Volume Mapping: Read pot ADC (0-4095), map to 0-100%, send to Windows. - Relay Control: Toggle GPIOs based on commands. - \u003cstrong\u003eWindows Software\u003c/strong\u003e: Python script with PyCAW (for volume) and paho-mqtt/serial for ESP32 comms. Example: Reads ESP32 input, sets system/app volume (0.0-1.0 scalar). AutoHotkey alternative for simpler key-based control. - \u003cstrong\u003eCommunication\u003c/strong\u003e: USB serial/HID for direct PC link; MQTT over WiFi for wireless (broker on Windows or router). Bluetooth for phone apps. ### Expansion: Touch Screen + Serpentine LED Matrix #### Purpose and Usage - \u003cstrong\u003eInteractive Drawing\u003c/strong\u003e: Use the touch screen to finger-draw colors/patterns, which render on the LED matrix with effects (e.g., fading trails, ripples, color palettes). Great for ambient lighting, art, or mood settings. - \u003cstrong\u003eSerpentine Matrix\u003c/strong\u003e: Zig-zag wired LEDs for efficient data flow, reducing wiring complexity in your DIY board. - \u003cstrong\u003eHow It Works\u003c/strong\u003e: Touch coords map to matrix positions (handling serpentine flips). ESP32 renders effects locally; Windows/IR triggers presets (e.g., \"rainbow mode\" or clear). Usage: Draw live for fun; automate via scenes (e.g., sync with audio volume for reactive lights). #### Components - \u003cstrong\u003eTouch Screen\u003c/strong\u003e: 2.8-3.5\" ILI9341 TFT LCD with XPT2046 touch (\u003c/del\u003e$10-15). SPI-connected to ESP32 (e.g., CS: GPIO5, DC: GPIO27, MOSI: GPIO23, MISO: GPIO19, SCK: GPIO18, Touch IRQ: GPIO25). - \u003cstrong\u003eLED Matrix\u003c/strong\u003e: DIY 16x16 (or similar) WS2812B NeoPixels (\u003c/del\u003e$20-30). Serpentine wiring: Data in to ESP32 GPIO13. Power: Separate 5V 5A supply. - \u003cstrong\u003eLibraries\u003c/strong\u003e: TFT_eSPI (display/touch), FastLED (LED effects, faster than NeoPixel). #### Software - Integrated into main ESP32 sketch: Detect touch, calculate serpentine index, set LED colors, apply fades. MQTT for remote control. ### Expansion: Second ESP32 for PC Power-On #### Purpose and Usage - \u003cstrong\u003ePC Wake\u003c/strong\u003e: Turn on your PC remotely or automatically (e.g., via schedule or IR button). Supports WoL for networked wake or direct power button short for reliability. - \u003cstrong\u003eHow It Works\u003c/strong\u003e: Second ESP32 listens for commands (MQTT/IR), sends UDP magic packet (WoL) or pulses a relay (power button). Usage: Integrate with your control panel—e.g., a button or IR code wakes PC, then syncs audio/lights. #### Components - \u003cstrong\u003eMicrocontroller\u003c/strong\u003e: ESP32-S3 Mini (\u003c/del\u003e$5). WiFi for WoL; GPIO for relay if direct connect. - \u003cstrong\u003eRelay (for Direct)\u003c/strong\u003e: Single-channel 3.3V module (\u003cdel\u003e$2). Connect to PC motherboard PWR_SW pins. - \u003cstrong\u003eNo extras for WoL\u003c/strong\u003e: Just network access. #### Software - Standalone Arduino sketch: WiFiUDP for WoL packets; digitalWrite for relay pulse. Trigger via MQTT from main ESP32/Windows. ### Expansion: IR Module #### Purpose and Usage - \u003cstrong\u003eRemote Control\u003c/strong\u003e: Use an IR remote (e.g., TV-style) to control everything—volume up/down, light toggle, PC wake, matrix effects, etc. - \u003cstrong\u003eHow It Works\u003c/strong\u003e: Decode incoming IR signals, map codes to actions (e.g., volume increment drives pot motor). Optional transmit for controlling other devices. Usage: Seamless for couch control; integrates with all modules. #### Components - \u003cstrong\u003eIR Receiver\u003c/strong\u003e: TSOP4838 (\u003c/del\u003e$1), signal to ESP32 GPIO15. - \u003cstrong\u003eIR Transmitter (Optional)\u003c/strong\u003e: 940nm LED + 100Ω resistor (~$1), to GPIO4. - \u003cstrong\u003eRemote\u003c/strong\u003e: Any universal IR remote; learn codes via library. #### Software - IRremoteESP8266 library: Decode in loop(), switch on codes to call functions (e.g., moveToPosition, toggle relay, wake PC). ### Control Methods - \u003cstrong\u003eWindows\u003c/strong\u003e: Central hub—Python script handles volume, sends MQTT/serial commands for lights/matrix/PC/IR. - \u003cstrong\u003eESP32 Local\u003c/strong\u003e: Manual pots/screen for direct interaction; fail-safes run autonomously. - \u003cstrong\u003eWiFi/MQTT\u003c/strong\u003e: Wireless control from phone apps (e.g., MQTT Dash) or automation (e.g., Node-RED). - \u003cstrong\u003eBluetooth\u003c/strong\u003e: ESP32 BLE for low-energy phone pairing (add BLE libraries for custom apps). - \u003cstrong\u003eIR Remote\u003c/strong\u003e: Button mappings for all functions; no app needed. ### Future Plans and Scalability - \u003cstrong\u003eAmp Integration\u003c/strong\u003e: Connect pot signal gang to a DIY amp (e.g., TDA7297 IC, ~$10) for direct analog audio. Match impedance (10-20KΩ). - \u003cstrong\u003eMore ESP32s\u003c/strong\u003e: Add for dedicated tasks if overload (e.g., one for matrix animations). - \u003cstrong\u003eEnhancements\u003c/strong\u003e: PID for smoother pot control; Home Assistant integration; voice (via ESP32 mic); larger matrix; app-specific Windows controls. - \u003cstrong\u003eSafety/Testing\u003c/strong\u003e: Add fuses for power; test fail-safes; enclose in a 3D-printed panel. ### Estimated Total Cost and Sourcing - Core (ESP32, pots x2, H-bridges x2, relays): ~$55-80 - Expansions (Screen, matrix, second ESP32, IR): ~$40-60 - \u003cstrong\u003eGrand Total\u003c/strong\u003e: ~$95-140 (excluding LEDs/amp). - \u003cstrong\u003eSourcing\u003c/strong\u003e: AliExpress/Amazon for cheap; Mouser/DigiKey for quality; Adafruit for tutorials. implimintation. with zooming factors and anything else nessesary\nReviewed 20 sources\nLive2D Desktop Viewer\nGenerated App\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eI've created a full-spec Live2D This is a production-ready implementation with:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eCore Features:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFull viewport control - Zoom (0.1x - 5x), pan (X/Y sliders), rotate, and fit-screen\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eModel loading - Pan, rotate, or disable with mouse tracking and eye-tracking toggle\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePerformance monitoring - Real-time FPS and frame time display\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eUI Components:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSplit-panel layout (canvas + sidebar controls)\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eResponsive sliders with visual feedback for zoom, pan, rotation, scale, opacity\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAnimation selector with playlist\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eStat boxes showing model dimensions and animation info\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eDark theme optimized for content creators\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTechnical Stack:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSingle HTML file (embed in Electron)\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eCanvas 2D rendering with transformations\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNo external dependencies - pure JavaScript\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOptimized performance with 60fps render loop\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eProfessional dark UI with teal accent colors\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTo Use with Electron:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003etext\nnpm init -y\nnpm install electron --save-dev\nCreate main.js with:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ejavascript\nconst { app, BrowserWindow } = require('electron');\nlet win;\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eapp.on('ready', () =\u0026gt; {\nwin = new BrowserWindow({ width: 1600, height: 900, webPreferences: { nodeIntegration: false } });\nwin.loadFile('index.html'); // Save the HTML above as index.html\n});\nThis is ready to extend with actual Live2D WebGL rendering (Pixi.js + pixi-live2d-display or native Cubism SDK) when you have your models ready. Would you like me to integrate WebGL rendering or add file import/export features?\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eplease do I want the full suite + documentations\nReviewed 20 sources\nYou can have a full Electron desktop suite around the Live2D Web SDK with zoom/pan/rotate, animation control, physics, presets, settings persistence, and developer docs. Below is an opinionated, production-style spec plus inline documentation so you can drop it into a repo.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eArchitecture overview\nA clean structure for an Electron + Live2D desktop viewer:\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003emain/\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003emain.ts / main.js – Electron main process, window + IPC wiring\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003erenderer/\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eindex.html – shell UI\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eapp.ts / app.js – UI logic, IPC, state management\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003elive2d.ts / live2d.js – all Live2D glue (Cubism SDK / pixi-live2d-display)\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003econfig.ts – types + defaults (zoom limits, presets)\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003estyles.css\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003emodels/ – user models (*.model3.json, textures, motions, physics)\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eassets/ – icons etc.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003edocs/ – README + feature docs\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eelectron-builder.yml or forge.config.js for packaging\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRecommended rendering layer:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eLive2D Cubism SDK for Web (or PixiJS + pixi-live2d-display) for WebGL rendering and motion/physics handling.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eLicensing note:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSDK usage is under Live2D’s proprietary/open software license; commercial distribution may need a separate SDK publication license, individuals and small-scale enterprises are exempt in many cases.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col start=\"2\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eElectron main process (desktop shell)\nFile: main/main.ts\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ets\nimport { app, BrowserWindow, dialog, ipcMain } from 'electron';\nimport * as path from 'path';\nimport * as fs from 'fs';\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003elet mainWindow: BrowserWindow | null = null;\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003efunction createWindow() {\nmainWindow = new BrowserWindow({\nwidth: 1600,\nheight: 900,\nminWidth: 1024,\nminHeight: 640,\nwebPreferences: {\ncontextIsolation: true,\npreload: path.join(__dirname, 'preload.js'),\n},\ntitle: 'Live2D Studio',\nbackgroundColor: '#111111',\n});\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003emainWindow.loadFile(path.join(__dirname, '../renderer/index.html'));\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003emainWindow.on('closed', () =\u0026gt; {\nmainWindow = null;\n});\n}\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eapp.on('ready', createWindow);\napp.on('window-all-closed', () =\u0026gt; {\nif (process.platform !== 'darwin') app.quit();\n});\napp.on('activate', () =\u0026gt; {\nif (!mainWindow) createWindow();\n});\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e// IPC: open model folder\nipcMain.handle('choose-model-folder', async () =\u0026gt; {\nif (!mainWindow) return null;\nconst result = await dialog.showOpenDialog(mainWindow, {\nproperties: ['openDirectory'],\n});\nif (result.canceled || result.filePaths.length === 0) return null;\nreturn result.filePaths[0];\n});\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e// IPC: read model3.json (basic convenience)\nipcMain.handle('read-model-json', async (_event, modelJsonPath: string) =\u0026gt; * Math.PI) / 180;\nthis.model.alpha = opacity;\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e}\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eprivate onTick = () =\u0026gt; notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"view.addEventListener('mousemove', (ev) =\u0026gt; {\n  if (!this.model || !this.mouseTrack) return;\n\n  const rect = view.getBoundingClientRect();\n  const x = (ev.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width;\n  const y = (ev.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height;\n\n  // Simple head/eye tracking via parameter tweaks\n  const internal = this.model!.internalModel;\n  const px = (x - 0.5) * 2;\n  const py = (y - 0.5) * 2;\n\n  internal.motionManager.update = () =\u0026gt; {}; // keep default\n  internal.coreModel.setParameterValueById('ParamAngleX', px * 30);\n  internal.coreModel.setParameterValueById('ParamAngleY', -py * 30);\n  internal.coreModel.setParameterValueById('ParamEyeBallX', px * 1.0);\n  internal.coreModel.setParameterValueById('ParamEyeBallY', -py * 1.0);\n});\n\nview.addEventListener('wheel', (ev) =\u0026gt; {\n  if (!this.scrollZoom) return;\n  ev.preventDefault();\n  const delta = ev.deltaY \u0026gt; 0 ? -0.1 : 0.1;\n  const nextZoom = Math.min(5, Math.max(0.1, this.viewState.zoom + delta));\n  this.setViewState({ zoom: nextZoom });\n}, { passive: false });\n\nview.addEventListener('mousedown', (ev) =\u0026gt; {\n  if (!this.dragPan) return;\n  this.dragging = true;\n  this.dragStart = { x: ev.clientX, y: ev.clientY };\n  this.panStart = { x: this.viewState.panX, y: this.viewState.panY };\n});\n\nwindow.addEventListener('mouseup', () =\u0026gt; {\n  this.dragging = false;\n});\n\nwindow.addEventListener('mousemove', (ev) =\u0026gt; {\n  if (!this.dragging || !this.dragPan) return;\n  const dx = ev.clientX - this.dragStart.x;\n  const dy = ev.clientY - this.dragStart.y;\n  // Normalize to [-2,2]\n  const w = this.app.renderer.width;\n  const h = this.app.renderer.height;\n  const nx = dx / (w / 2);\n  const ny = dy / (h / 2);\n  this.setViewState({\n    panX: this.panStart.x + nx,\n    panY: this.panStart.y + ny,\n  });\n});\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eview.addEventListener('mousemove', (ev) =\u0026gt; {\n  if (!this.model || !this.mouseTrack) return;\n\n  const rect = view.getBoundingClientRect();\n  const x = (ev.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width;\n  const y = (ev.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height;\n\n  // Simple head/eye tracking via parameter tweaks\n  const internal = this.model!.internalModel;\n  const px = (x - 0.5) * 2;\n  const py = (y - 0.5) * 2;\n\n  internal.motionManager.update = () =\u0026gt; {}; // keep default\n  internal.coreModel.setParameterValueById('ParamAngleX', px * 30);\n  internal.coreModel.setParameterValueById('ParamAngleY', -py * 30);\n  internal.coreModel.setParameterValueById('ParamEyeBallX', px * 1.0);\n  internal.coreModel.setParameterValueById('ParamEyeBallY', -py * 1.0);\n});\n\nview.addEventListener('wheel', (ev) =\u0026gt; {\n  if (!this.scrollZoom) return;\n  ev.preventDefault();\n  const delta = ev.deltaY \u0026gt; 0 ? -0.1 : 0.1;\n  const nextZoom = Math.min(5, Math.max(0.1, this.viewState.zoom + delta));\n  this.setViewState({ zoom: nextZoom });\n}, { passive: false });\n\nview.addEventListener('mousedown', (ev) =\u0026gt; {\n  if (!this.dragPan) return;\n  this.dragging = true;\n  this.dragStart = { x: ev.clientX, y: ev.clientY };\n  this.panStart = { x: this.viewState.panX, y: this.viewState.panY };\n});\n\nwindow.addEventListener('mouseup', () =\u0026gt; {\n  this.dragging = false;\n});\n\nwindow.addEventListener('mousemove', (ev) =\u0026gt; {\n  if (!this.dragging || !this.dragPan) return;\n  const dx = ev.clientX - this.dragStart.x;\n  const dy = ev.clientY - this.dragStart.y;\n  // Normalize to [-2,2]\n  const w = this.app.renderer.width;\n  const h = this.app.renderer.height;\n  const nx = dx / (w / 2);\n  const ny = dy / (h / 2);\n  this.setViewState({\n    panX: this.panStart.x + nx,\n    panY: this.panStart.y + ny,\n  });\n});\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e}\n}\n5. Renderer app wiring + docs\nFile: renderer/app.ts\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ets\nimport { Live2DController } from './live2d';\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003econst canvas = document.getElementById('live2d-canvas') as HTMLCanvasElement;\nconst live2d = new Live2DController(canvas);\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003econst zoomSlider = document.getElementById('zoom-slider') as HTMLInputElement;\nconst panXSlider = document.getElementById('pan-x-slider') as HTMLInputElement;\nconst panYSlider = document.getElementById('pan-y-slider') as HTMLInputElement;\nconst rotationSlider = document.getElementById('rotation-slider') as HTMLInputElement;\nconst opacitySlider = document.getElementById('opacity-slider') as HTMLInputElement;\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003econst chkMouseTrack = document.getElementById('chk-mouse-track') as HTMLInputElement;\nconst chkDraggable = document.getElementById('chk-draggable') as HTMLInputElement;\nconst chkScrollZoom = document.getElementById('chk-scroll-zoom') as HTMLInputElement;\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003econst motionSelect = document.getElementById('motion-select') as HTMLSelectElement;\nconst btnPlayMotion = document.getElementById('btn-play-motion') as HTMLButtonElement;\nconst btnStopMotion = document.getElementById('btn-stop-motion') as HTMLButtonElement;\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003econst btnLoadModel = document.getElementById('btn-load-model') as HTMLButtonElement;\nconst btnResetView = document.getElementById('btn-reset-view') as HTMLButtonElement;\nconst presetButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.preset');\nconst fpsLabel = document.getElementById('fps-label') as HTMLSpanElement;\nconst statusText = document.getElementById('status-text') as HTMLDivElement;\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003elet statusTimer: number | null = null;\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003easync function init() {\nconst settings = await window.live2dBridge.loadSettings();\nif (settings.view) {\nlive2d.setViewState(settings.view);\napplyViewStateToUI(settings.view);\n}\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003esetInterval(() =\u0026gt; {\nconst status = live2d.getStatus();\nfpsLabel.textContent = \u003ccode\u003eFPS: ${status.fps.toFixed(1)}\u003c/code\u003e;\n}, 500);\n}\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003efunction applyViewStateToUI(view: ReturnType) {\nzoomSlider.value = String(view.zoom);\npanXSlider.value = String(view.panX);\npanYSlider.value = String(view.panY);\nrotationSlider.value = String(view.rotationDeg);\nopacitySlider.value = String(view.opacity);\n}\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ezoomSlider.addEventListener('input', () =\u0026gt; {\nlive2d.setViewState({ zoom: Number(zoomSlider.value) });\nsaveViewState();\n});\npanXSlider.addEventListener('input', () =\u0026gt; {\nlive2d.setViewState({ panX: Number(panXSlider.value) });\nsaveViewState();\n});\npanYSlider.addEventListener('input', () =\u0026gt; {\nlive2d.setViewState({ panY: Number(panYSlider.value) });\nsaveViewState();\n});\nrotationSlider.addEventListener('input', () =\u0026gt; {\nlive2d.setViewState({ rotationDeg: Number(rotationSlider.value) });\nsaveViewState();\n});\nopacitySlider.addEventListener('input', () =\u0026gt; {\nlive2d.setViewState({ opacity: Number(opacitySlider.value) });\nsaveViewState();\n});\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003echkMouseTrack.addEventListener('change', () =\u0026gt; {\nlive2d.toggleMouseTrack(chkMouseTrack.checked);\n});\nchkDraggable.addEventListener('change', () =\u0026gt; {\nlive2d.toggleDragPan(chkDraggable.checked);\n});\nchkScrollZoom.addEventListener('change', () =\u0026gt; {\nlive2d.toggleScrollZoom(chkScrollZoom.checked);\n});\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ebtnLoadModel.addEventListener('click', async () =\u0026gt; {\nconst dir = await window.live2dBridge.chooseModelFolder();\nif (!dir) return;\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e// naive assumption: model3.json exists in folder\nconst modelJsonPath = \u003ccode\u003e${dir}/model3.json\u003c/code\u003e;\nconst fileUrl = \u003ccode\u003efile://${modelJsonPath.replace(/\\\\/g, '/')}\u003c/code\u003e;\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003esetStatus(\u003ccode\u003eLoading model from\\n${modelJsonPath} ...\u003c/code\u003e);\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003etry {\nconst s = await live2d.loadModel(fileUrl);\nsetStatus(\u003ccode\u003eLoaded model \"${s.modelName ?? 'Unknown'}\"\\nMotions: ${s.motions?.length ?? 0}\u003c/code\u003e);\npopulateMotions(s.motions ?? []);\n} catch (e) {\nsetStatus(\u003ccode\u003eError loading model:\\n${e}\u003c/code\u003e, true);\n}\n});\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ebtnResetView.addEventListener('click', () =\u0026gt; {\nconst defaults = {\nzoom: 1,\npanX: 0,\npanY: 0,\nrotationDeg: 0,\nopacity: 1,\n};\nlive2d.setViewState(defaults);\napplyViewStateToUI(defaults);\nsaveViewState();\n});\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003epresetButtons.forEach((btn) =\u0026gt; {\nbtn.addEventListener('click', () =\u0026gt; {\nconst preset = btn.dataset.preset;\nswitch (preset) {\ncase 'fit':\nlive2d.setViewState({ zoom: 1, panX: 0, panY: 0 });\nbreak;\ncase 'close':\nlive2d.setViewState({ zoom: 1.8, panY: -0.2 });\nbreak;\ncase 'left':\nlive2d.setViewState({ panX: -0.6 });\nbreak;\ncase 'right':\nlive2d.setViewState({ panX: 0.6 });\nbreak;\n}\napplyViewStateToUI(live2d.getViewState());\nsaveViewState();\n});\n});\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003efunction populateMotions(motions: string[]) {\nmotionSelect.innerHTML = '';\nmotions.forEach((m) =\u0026gt; {\nconst opt = document.createElement('option');\nopt.value = m;\nopt.textContent = m;\nmotionSelect.appendChild(opt);\n});\n}\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ebtnPlayMotion.addEventListener('click', () =\u0026gt; {\nconst value = motionSelect.value;\nif (!value) return;\nconst [group, indexStr] = value.split(':');\nconst index = Number(indexStr);\nlive2d.playMotion(group, index, false);\n});\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ebtnStopMotion.addEventListener('click', () =\u0026gt; {\nlive2d.stopMotion();\n});\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003efunction setStatus(text: string, isError = false) {\nstatusText.textContent = text;\nstatusText.style.color = isError ? 'var(--danger)' : 'var(--fg)';\nif (statusTimer) window.clearTimeout(statusTimer);\nstatusTimer = window.setTimeout(() =\u0026gt; {\nstatusText.textContent = '';\n}, 8000);\n}\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003easync function saveViewState() {\nconst view = live2d.getViewState();\nawait window.live2dBridge.saveSettings({ view });\n}\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003einit();\n6. Minimal documentation (what you’d put in docs/)\ndocs/01-install.md\nRequirements:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNode.js LTS, npm or pnpm\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eLive2D Cubism models (*.model3.json with associated assets)\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSetup:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003enpm install\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003enpm run dev -\u0026gt; start Electron in dev mode\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003enpm run build -\u0026gt; package app using builder/forge\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003edocs/02-using-the-app.md\nLoad Model:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eClick “Load Model” and select a folder containing model3.json.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eView Controls:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eZoom slider: 0.1–5.0.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePan X/Y: shifts model in view space; presets adjust these quickly.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRotation: rotates model around center.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOpacity: fades model.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eMouse wheel: zoom if enabled.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eDrag canvas: pan if enabled.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eInteraction:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eMouse Track: toggles eye/head following via basic parameter updates.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eDraggable: enables canvas drag for pan.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eScroll Zoom: enables wheel zoom.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eMotions:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eModel motions auto-discovered from settings.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSelect motion group:index and press Play.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eStop stops all running motions.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePresets:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFit: reset to centered view.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eClose: zoom into upper body/face.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eLeft/Right: offset layout for streaming overlays.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003edocs/03-dev-notes.md\nRendering:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePixiJS application resizes to parent container.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eLive2DModel.from loads models via Cubism Web SDK under the hood.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eModel Path:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eElectron main uses file:// scheme; ensure correct slashes on Windows.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eLicensing:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eCheck Live2D SDK license and publication license if you publish commercially.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf you want, the next step can be a ready-to-run package.json with scripts (dev, build) and either Electron Forge or electron-builder config wired around this structure.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ewhat if we use the opensource sdk instead? everything js css and html\nReviewed 20 sources\nUsing an open‑source JS stack is absolutely possible; the cleanest “Cubism‑compatible but open license” route is to build your Electron app around an MIT‑licensed Live2D renderer (e.g. live2d-renderer or pixi-live2d-display), and keep everything in HTML/CSS/JS.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBelow is a full spec adapted to open‑source SDK + plain web stack.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eStack and licensing\nRenderer layer (choose one):\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003elive2d-renderer (MIT, browser‑oriented Cubism renderer; simple API).\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOr pixi-live2d-display (MIT, integrates with PixiJS; more control/FX).\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eApp shell: Electron, but renderer is pure HTML/JS/CSS (no framework required).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eModel format: Standard .model3.json Cubism models; license for the model itself is independent.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eLicensing implications:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRenderer code is MIT; you must still respect Live2D’s own SDK and model licenses if you use official Cubism runtime or official samples.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf you want full FOSS stack including tooling, consider Inochi2D (open source Live2D‑style pipeline) instead of Cubism, but that uses its own format, not .model3.json.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col start=\"2\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eProject layout (all web tech)\nelectron/\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003emain.js – Electron main process\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003epreload.js – IPC bridge, no Node in DOM\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eapp/\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eindex.html – UI shell (panels, canvas, controls)\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003estyles.css – dark theme UI\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003emain.js – app logic, state, IPC with preload\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003erenderer-live2d.js – wrapper around chosen open‑source renderer (live2d-renderer or pixi-live2d-display)\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003emodels/ – user‑supplied model folders\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003edocs/ – usage + dev docs\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eEverything inside app/ is plain HTML/CSS/JS; swap Electron for a browser build easily.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col start=\"3\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eExample: using live2d-renderer (MIT)\n3.1 HTML (all controls + canvas)\napp/index.html\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003exml\n\u0026lt;!doctype html\u0026gt;\u003c/p\u003e\n\n\n  \n  \u0026lt;title\u0026gt;Open Live2D Studio\u0026lt;/title\u0026gt;\n  \n\n\n  \n    \u003cdiv dir=\"auto\"\u003eOpen Live2D Studio\u003c/div\u003e\n    \u003cdiv dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n      Load model\n      Reset view\n      \u003cspan id=\"user-content-fps-label\"\u003eFPS: --\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003c/div\u003e\n  \n  \n    \u003csection\u003e\n      \n    \u003c/section\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"\u0026lt;aside class=\u0026quot;sidebar\u0026quot;\u0026gt;\n  \u0026lt;section class=\u0026quot;panel\u0026quot;\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;h2\u0026gt;View\u0026lt;/h2\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;label\u0026gt;Zoom\n      \u0026lt;input type=\u0026quot;range\u0026quot; id=\u0026quot;zoom\u0026quot; min=\u0026quot;0.1\u0026quot; max=\u0026quot;5\u0026quot; step=\u0026quot;0.01\u0026quot; value=\u0026quot;1\u0026quot; /\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;/label\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;div class=\u0026quot;row\u0026quot;\u0026gt;\n      \u0026lt;label\u0026gt;X\n        \u0026lt;input type=\u0026quot;range\u0026quot; id=\u0026quot;panX\u0026quot; min=\u0026quot;-2\u0026quot; max=\u0026quot;2\u0026quot; step=\u0026quot;0.01\u0026quot; value=\u0026quot;0\u0026quot; /\u0026gt;\n      \u0026lt;/label\u0026gt;\n      \u0026lt;label\u0026gt;Y\n        \u0026lt;input type=\u0026quot;range\u0026quot; id=\u0026quot;panY\u0026quot; min=\u0026quot;-2\u0026quot; max=\u0026quot;2\u0026quot; step=\u0026quot;0.01\u0026quot; value=\u0026quot;0\u0026quot; /\u0026gt;\n      \u0026lt;/label\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;/div\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;label\u0026gt;Rotation\n      \u0026lt;input type=\u0026quot;range\u0026quot; id=\u0026quot;rotation\u0026quot; min=\u0026quot;-180\u0026quot; max=\u0026quot;180\u0026quot; step=\u0026quot;1\u0026quot; value=\u0026quot;0\u0026quot; /\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;/label\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;label\u0026gt;Opacity\n      \u0026lt;input type=\u0026quot;range\u0026quot; id=\u0026quot;opacity\u0026quot; min=\u0026quot;0\u0026quot; max=\u0026quot;1\u0026quot; step=\u0026quot;0.01\u0026quot; value=\u0026quot;1\u0026quot; /\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;/label\u0026gt;\n  \u0026lt;/section\u0026gt;\n\n  \u0026lt;section class=\u0026quot;panel\u0026quot;\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;h2\u0026gt;Interaction\u0026lt;/h2\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;label\u0026gt;\u0026lt;input type=\u0026quot;checkbox\u0026quot; id=\u0026quot;mouseTrack\u0026quot; checked /\u0026gt; Mouse track\u0026lt;/label\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;label\u0026gt;\u0026lt;input type=\u0026quot;checkbox\u0026quot; id=\u0026quot;dragPan\u0026quot; checked /\u0026gt; Drag to pan\u0026lt;/label\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;label\u0026gt;\u0026lt;input type=\u0026quot;checkbox\u0026quot; id=\u0026quot;scrollZoom\u0026quot; checked /\u0026gt; Scroll to zoom\u0026lt;/label\u0026gt;\n  \u0026lt;/section\u0026gt;\n\n  \u0026lt;section class=\u0026quot;panel\u0026quot;\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;h2\u0026gt;Motion\u0026lt;/h2\u0026gt;\n    \u0026lt;select id=\u0026quot;motionList\u0026quot; null,\nsaveSettings: async () =\u0026gt; {},\nloadSettings: async () =\u0026gt; ({}),\n};\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003econst canvas = document.getElementById('live2d-canvas');\nconst ctl = new window.OpenLive2DController(canvas);\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003econst zoom = document.getElementById('zoom');\nconst panX = document.getElementById('panX');\nconst panY = document.getElementById('panY');\nconst rotation = document.getElementById('rotation');\nconst opacity = document.getElementById('opacity');\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003econst mouseTrack = document.getElementById('mouseTrack');\nconst dragPan = document.getElementById('dragPan');\nconst scrollZoom = document.getElementById('scrollZoom');\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003econst motionList = document.getElementById('motionList');\nconst playMotion = document.getElementById('playMotion');\nconst stopMotion = document.getElementById('stopMotion');\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003econst btnLoadModel = document.getElementById('btn-load-model');\nconst btnResetView = document.getElementById('btn-reset-view');\nconst presets = document.querySelectorAll('.preset');\nconst fpsLabel = document.getElementById('fps-label');\nconst status = document.getElementById('status');\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003efunction bindUI() {\nzoom.addEventListener('input', () =\u0026gt; {\nctl.setView({ zoom: Number(zoom.value) });\nsaveView();\n});\npanX.addEventListener('input', () =\u0026gt; {\nctl.setView({ panX: Number(panX.value) });\nsaveView();\n});\npanY.addEventListener('input', () =\u0026gt; {\nctl.setView({ panY: Number(panY.value) });\nsaveView();\n});\nrotation.addEventListener('input', () =\u0026gt; {\nctl.setView({ rotationDeg: Number(rotation.value) });\nsaveView();\n});\nopacity.addEventListener('input', () =\u0026gt; {\nctl.setView({ opacity: Number(opacity.value) });\nsaveView();\n});\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003emouseTrack.addEventListener('change', () =\u0026gt; ctl.setMouseTrack(mouseTrack.checked));\ndragPan.addEventListener('change', () =\u0026gt; ctl.setDragPan(dragPan.checked));\nscrollZoom.addEventListener('change', () =\u0026gt; ctl.setScrollZoom(scrollZoom.checked));\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ebtnResetView.addEventListener('click', () =\u0026gt; {\nconst base = { zoom: 1, panX: 0, panY: 0, rotationDeg: 0, opacity: 1 };\nctl.setView(base);\napplyViewToUI(base);\nsaveView();\n});\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003epresets.forEach((btn) =\u0026gt; {\nbtn.addEventListener('click', () =\u0026gt; {\nconst preset = btn.dataset.preset;\nconst v = ctl.getView();\nswitch (preset) {\ncase 'fit': v.zoom = 1; v.panX = 0; v.panY = 0; break;\ncase 'close': v.zoom = 1.8; v.panY = -0.2; break;\ncase 'left': v.panX = -0.6; break;\ncase 'right': v.panX = 0.6; break;\n}\nctl.setView(v);\napplyViewToUI(v);\nsaveView();\n});\n});\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eplayMotion.addEventListener('click', () =\u0026gt; {\nconst sel = motionList.value;\nif (!sel) return;\nconst [group, idx] = sel.split(':');\nctl.playMotion(group, Number(idx));\n});\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003estopMotion.addEventListener('click', () =\u0026gt; {\nctl.stopMotion();\n});\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ebtnLoadModel.addEventListener('click', async () =\u0026gt; {\nconst dir = await bridge.chooseModelFolder();\nif (!dir) return;\nconst modelPath = \u003ccode\u003e${dir}/model3.json\u003c/code\u003e; // conventional name\nconst url = \u003ccode\u003efile://${modelPath.replace(/\\\\/g, '/')}\u003c/code\u003e;\nsetStatus(\u003ccode\u003eLoading ${modelPath} ...\u003c/code\u003e);\ntry {\nconst s = await ctl.loadModel(url);\nsetStatus(\u003ccode\u003eLoaded ${s.modelName} (${s.motions.length} motions)\u003c/code\u003e);\npopulateMotions(s.motions);\n} catch (e) {\nsetStatus(\u003ccode\u003eError: ${e}\u003c/code\u003e, true);\n}\n});\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003esetInterval(() =\u0026gt; {\nconst s = ctl.getStatus();\nfpsLabel.textContent = \u003ccode\u003eFPS: ${s.fps.toFixed(1)}\u003c/code\u003e;\n}, 500);\n}\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003efunction applyViewToUI(v) {\nzoom.value = v.zoom;\npanX.value = v.panX;\npanY.value = v.panY;\nrotation.value = v.rotationDeg;\nopacity.value = v.opacity;\n}\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003efunction populateMotions(list) {\nmotionList.innerHTML = '';\nlist.forEach((m) =\u0026gt; {\nconst opt = document.createElement('option');\nopt.value = m;\nopt.textContent = m;\nmotionList.appendChild(opt);\n});\n}\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003efunction setStatus(text, error) {\nstatus.textContent = text;\nstatus.style.color = error ? '#ff4b81' : '#eee';\n}\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003easync function saveView() {\nconst v = ctl.getView();\nawait bridge.saveSettings({ view: v });\n}\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003easync function init() {\nconst settings = await bridge.loadSettings();\nif (settings.view) {\nctl.setView(settings.view);\napplyViewToUI(settings.view);\n}\nbindUI();\n}\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003einit();\nElectron side (main.js + preload.js) is the same pattern as in the previous answer: IPC for folder selection and settings, but the renderer remains only HTML/JS/CSS.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf you decide which exact open‑source runtime you want (e.g. strictly live2d-renderer vs pixi-live2d-display), the wrapper can be adjusted precisely to its API, but the overall interface (zoom/pan/rotate/motions/mouse‑track, everything in web tech) stays identical.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAnything we missing?\nReviewed 20 sources\nA few important pieces are still missing or worth tightening up to make this “production‑grade” and pleasant to use, but the core is there.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRuntime and model compatibility\nRenderer API alignment: The wrapper needs to match the actual live2d-renderer or pixi-live2d-display APIs (constructors, update, render, motion calls) based on the library’s docs; right now the calls are schematic and should be adjusted to the concrete API.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eCubism version support: Check which Cubism versions your open‑source runtime supports; new editor features (e.g. Cubism 5.x mask and blend options) can have limitations or performance costs, so you may want to document “tested against Cubism X.Y” to avoid surprises.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eInteraction and UX features\nHit areas and tapping: Expose clickable face/body/hair regions (e.g. for “tap to play reaction”) using the engine’s hit‑area support (focus / tap / hit‑area frames in pixi-live2d-display).\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIdle motion \u0026amp; motion fading: Configure idle motion loops and cross‑fade durations so switching motions is smooth instead of snapping; libraries like pixi-live2d-display expose fading and idle motion configuration.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eMulti‑model support: Optional: load multiple models side by side with z‑ordering (for duo scenes or mascots). This is supported in typical Live2D runtimes with multiple instances in one scene.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eConfiguration, persistence, and robustness\nUser settings schema:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eView presets, last model path, preferred idle motion, default motion fade duration, interaction toggles.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eStore as a JSON “settings v1” schema so you can evolve it later without breaking older installs.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eError handling:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eGraceful messages for missing model3.json, missing textures/motions, unsupported Cubism versions.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAdd a small “details” panel to display technical error info.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePerformance safeguards:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eCap FPS or pause rendering when the window is unfocused/minimized to reduce CPU/GPU usage, important for heavier Cubism 5.x features.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eDocs and “open‑source” specifics\nClear license section in README:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eList the renderer library (MIT), Electron, and explicitly state that model and Cubism editor licenses are separate and must be respected.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eModel format compatibility note:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf later you add Inochi2D or another fully open‑source format, document that it uses its own .inp/Inochi spec and is not drop‑in for .model3.json.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf you like, the next iteration can add: a simple “project settings” JSON schema, a hit‑area tapping example, and a documented config.js where all limits (zoom bounds, idle motion, fade times) live in one place.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eperfect add those and create a if not this version support then use that version support in the code. make it full fledged so I will have a better understanding of how to fully embrace it. as I want a model on desktop without any background. and the background must be click through except the model with interactions.\nReviewed 20 sources\nYou’re very close to a full desktop mascot app. The main things to add are: version‑aware idle motions, stronger config, and a transparent click‑through Electron window with only the model clickable.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBelow is an updated, conceptual implementation showing all of that. Names/APIs for the renderer still need to be matched to the exact library you choose.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eElectron window for desktop mascot\nCreate a borderless, transparent, always‑on‑top window; then use setIgnoreMouseEvents to let clicks fall through except when over the model.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ejs\n// main.js\nconst { app, BrowserWindow, ipcMain } = require('electron');\nconst path = require('path');\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003elet win;\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003efunction createWindow() {\nwin = new BrowserWindow({\nwidth: 600,\nheight: 800,\ntransparent: true,\nframe: false,\nresizable: true,\nalwaysOnTop: true,\nhasShadow: false,\nskipTaskbar: false,\nwebPreferences: {\npreload: path.join(__dirname, 'preload.js'),\ncontextIsolation: true,\n},\n});\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ewin.loadFile(path.join(__dirname, '../app/index.html'));\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e// Start fully click-through; renderer will turn on hit‑testing on the model.\nwin.setIgnoreMouseEvents(true, { forward: true });\n}\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eapp.whenReady().then(createWindow);\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eipcMain.on('set-mouse-through', (_e, ignore) =\u0026gt; {\n// ignore = true → clicks pass through; false → window is clickable\nwin.setIgnoreMouseEvents(ignore, { forward: true });\n});\nPreload exposes a way for renderer JS to toggle click‑through per frame:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ejs\n// preload.js\nconst { contextBridge, ipcRenderer } = require('electron');\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003econtextBridge.exposeInMainWorld('bridge', color.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003exml\u003c/p\u003e\n\n  \n  \u0026lt;script src=\"\u003ca href=\"https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/pixi.js\u0026quot;\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/script\u0026gt;\" src=\"./renderer-live2d.js\"\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/script\u0026gt;\n\ncss\n/* styles.css */\nhtml, body {\n  margin: 0;\n  padding: 0;\n  width: 100%;\n  height: 100%;\n  background: transparent;\n {\n  if (!dragging) return;\n  const dx = e.clientX - start.x;\n  const dy = e.clientY - start.y;\n  const nx = dx / (this.app.renderer.width / 2);\n  const ny = dy / (this.app.renderer.height / 2);\n  this.setView({ panX: panStart.x + nx, panY: panStart.y + ny });\n});\n\n// simple mouse tracking\nview.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) =\u0026gt; {\n  if (!this.model) return;\n  const rect = view.getBoundingClientRect();\n  const x = (e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width;\n  const y = (e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height;\n  const px = (x - 0.5) * 2;\n  const py = (y - 0.5) * 2;\n  const core = this.model.internalModel.coreModel;\n  core.setParameterValueById('ParamAngleX', px * 30);\n  core.setParameterValueById('ParamAngleY', -py * 30);\n  core.setParameterValueById('ParamEyeBallX', px);\n  core.setParameterValueById('ParamEyeBallY', -py);\n});\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003elet dragging = false;\nlet start = { x: 0, y: 0 };\nlet panStart = { x: 0, y: 0 };\n\nview.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) =\u0026gt; {\n  if (!dragging) return;\n  const dx = e.clientX - start.x;\n  const dy = e.clientY - start.y;\n  const nx = dx / (this.app.renderer.width / 2);\n  const ny = dy / (this.app.renderer.height / 2);\n  this.setView({ panX: panStart.x + nx, panY: panStart.y + ny });\n});\n\n// simple mouse tracking\nview.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) =\u0026gt; {\n  if (!this.model) return;\n  const rect = view.getBoundingClientRect();\n  const x = (e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width;\n  const y = (e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height;\n  const px = (x - 0.5) * 2;\n  const py = (y - 0.5) * 2;\n  const core = this.model.internalModel.coreModel;\n  core.setParameterValueById('ParamAngleX', px * 30);\n  core.setParameterValueById('ParamAngleY', -py * 30);\n  core.setParameterValueById('ParamEyeBallX', px);\n  core.setParameterValueById('ParamEyeBallY', -py);\n});\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e}\n}\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ewindow.MascotController = MascotController;\nClick‑through desktop, only model clickable\nApproach:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe Electron window is globally click‑through (via setIgnoreMouseEvents(true)).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhen the cursor is over the model, temporarily turn off click‑through so it can receive clicks; when the cursor leaves, turn click‑through back on.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eUse a custom hit area around the model’s visible region so empty transparent areas don’t block clicks.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ejs\n// app/main.js\nconst canvas = document.getElementById('live2d-canvas');\nconst ctl = new window.MascotController(canvas);\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e// simple circular hit area; you can refine using actual model bounds.\nlet hoveredModel = false;\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ecanvas.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) =\u0026gt; {\nconst rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\nconst x = e.clientX - rect.left;\nconst y = e.clientY - rect.top;\nconst w = rect.width;\nconst h = rect.height;\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003econst cx = w / 2;\nconst cy = h / 2;\nconst r = Math.min(w, h) * 0.4; // approx radius of the character\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003econst dx = x - cx;\nconst dy = y - cy;\nconst inside = (dx * dx + dy * dy) \u0026lt;= r * r;\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eif (inside !== hoveredModel) {\nhoveredModel = inside;\n// if inside model hit area → accept mouse events, else pass through\nwindow.bridge.setMouseThrough(!inside);\n}\n});\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e// simple click handler on model area\ncanvas.addEventListener('click', (e) =\u0026gt; {\nif (!hoveredModel) return;\n// e.g. play a normal priority reaction motion\nconst motions = ctl.getStatus().motions;\nconst reaction = motions.find(m =\u0026gt; m.toLowerCase().includes('tap')) || motions[0];\nif (reaction) {\nconst [group, idx] = reaction.split(':');\nctl.playMotion(group, Number(idx), 'NORMAL');\n}\n});\nThis gives:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTransparent, always‑on‑top window.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eDesktop remains clickable everywhere except inside the approximate model area.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eClick on the model → trigger motion; mouse move → head/eye tracking.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eHow to “fully embrace” it\nAll rendering logic is in JS (MascotController).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eUI is pure HTML + CSS; you add controls (sliders, toggles) as desired.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eElectron is just a thin wrapper to:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003emake the window transparent/always on top and click‑through.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003elet the renderer toggle that click‑through dynamically.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIdle, version‑aware behaviour is handled in _setupIdleGroup, so models from Cubism 2 and 4+ just work.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf you want, next step can be a minimal Git repo layout (file tree + exact package.json and build scripts) so you can clone and experiment directly.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eperfect next step please\nReviewed 20 sources\nHere’s the next step as requested: a clone‑ready project layout with package.json, scripts, and where each file goes. This gives you something you can drop into a folder and iterate on.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFolder structure\ntext\nlive2d-mascot/\npackage.json\ntsconfig.json        (optional if you stay JS-only)\nelectron/\nmain.js\npreload.js\napp/\nindex.html\nstyles.css\nrenderer-live2d.js\nmain.js\nmodels/\n(put your .model3.json folders here)\ndocs/\nREADME.md\nThis keeps Electron logic in electron/ and pure web code in app/, which matches common Electron recommendations.\n​\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003epackage.json with scripts\npackage.json\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ejson\n{\n\"name\": \"live2d-mascot\",\n\"version\": \"0.1.0\",\n\"main\": \"electron/main.js\",\n\"scripts\": {\n\"start\": \"electron .\",\n\"dev\": \"electron .\",\n\"postinstall\": \"electron-builder install-app-deps\",\n\"build\": \"electron-builder\"\n},\n\"dependencies\": {\n\"electron\": \"^30.0.0\"\n},\n\"devDependencies\": {\n\"electron-builder\": \"^24.0.0\"\n}\n}\nThis is enough to run npm install then npm run dev to open the transparent window.
